# engineering-design-generator-bootstrap
## Requirements
- `native-design-generation`: planEngineeringDesign constructs the original inference-market request for the selected high-level or low-level stage; receiveEngineeringDesign receives the actual delivered candidate against that exact plan and current source cut. The returned plan and received candidate are the two behavioral products; original owner composition and effect constraints have separate mandatory review obligations. [claims: bootstrap-scope, declared-interface]
- `exact-source-cut`: Check only this structural projection of the incumbent source records plus lld-upstream. sources has exactly requirement, specification and architecture for high-level, and those plus high-level for low-level, each kind once. Parse each exact text as a nonnull nonarray JSON object; malformed source yields TypeError. All source, deliveryText, inputSchemaJson and outputSchemaJson parsing uses the unmodified native JSON.parse with no reviver. Native accepted syntax, JSON whitespace and duplicate-object-member last-member-wins semantics apply before the stated structural checks; do not add a duplicate-member detector, alternate parser, canonical-text requirement or extra syntax refusal. Duplicate raw JSON member names are distinct from the explicitly forbidden duplicate ID/port/trace array values, which are still checked on the parsed record. Exact source text hashing retains the entire supplied text even when two texts parse to the same object. The selected original Data supplier still determines semantic identity and its own TypeError refusals. Propagate a TypeError thrown by that supplier as TypeError from planning/receiving source reconstruction, including when an ignored projection field is outside its admitted JSON-to-SEN domain. In particular an otherwise valid complete Requirement with an extra member parsed from positive or negative JSON exponent overflow produces a supplier TypeError, so the operation also throws TypeError. Unlisted fields are ignored by this structural projection, but are part of the COMPLETE object passed to the supplier; never strip, repair or JSON-roundtrip them before identity. These exact installed-supplier boundary observations are evidence, not a new identity algorithm: [{"label":"positive-overflow","literal":"1e400","outcome":"refused","name":"TypeError","code":"non-relational-content","message":"value must cross the admitted JSON-to-SEN boundary"},{"label":"negative-overflow","literal":"-1e400","outcome":"refused","name":"TypeError","code":"non-relational-content","message":"value must cross the admitted JSON-to-SEN boundary"},{"label":"negative-zero","literal":"-0","outcome":"refused","name":"TypeError","code":"non-relational-content","message":"value must cross the admitted JSON-to-SEN boundary"},{"label":"underflow-to-zero","literal":"1e-400","outcome":"identified","address":"ni:///sha-256;NiJ0kj4ES1eLtQclH3ayQi-n9Mkw8c1nHoWEM04s4dw"},{"label":"unpaired-high-surrogate","literal":"\"\\ud800\"","outcome":"identified","address":"ni:///sha-256;zMnRfcpQZPylhO-E8T_CvsOXU2w9sAiOTxBEIovS-Hw"},{"label":"unpaired-low-surrogate","literal":"\"\\udc00\"","outcome":"identified","address":"ni:///sha-256;lJ7Ln8JbEgKfWgbdsvZllMtYxPCjoHcRgdDQQg_5L2o"}]. Do not reimplement or attempt to requalify the declared Data supplier internals in this bootstrap; its native boundary and supplied fixtures are the dependency contract. The following complete-input observation fixtures resolve the whole objects identified above. Each requirementSourceText is a COMPLETE original Requirement JSON source string, and the actual supplier input is exactly JSON.parse(requirementSourceText), not the wrapper. the input value.\"}],\"extra\":\"\\udc00\"}","outcome":"identified","address":"ni:///sha-256;lJ7Ln8JbEgKfWgbdsvZllMtYxPCjoHcRgdDQQg_5L2o"}]. Each recorded identified address and each refusal was reobtained by executing the original installed supplier on that exact parsed complete source. Nonblank means a string with nonempty trim(), while retaining its exact spelling. Requirement.kind is requirement, id is a nonblank string matching the exact incumbent Requirement grammar ^[a-z0-9]+(?:-[a-z0-9]+)*$ (ASCII lowercase letters/digits, optional single hyphen separators, no leading/trailing/doubled hyphens; an id without any hyphen is allowed), and met-when is a nonempty array of objects with unique nonblank cell starting with id plus colon, target held and nonblank says containing no CR (CARRIAGE RETURN), LF (LINE FEED) or NUL (NULL), matching the incumbent source condition rule. Unicode NEXT LINE, LINE SEPARATOR and PARAGRAPH SEPARATOR are permitted in says when its trim() is nonempty; do not add a line-break exclusion beyond those three characters. Specification uses the ORIGINAL identity.id, topics[].clauses and provenance.derivedFrom carrier, not a new type/id/flat-clauses dialect. identity.id is nonblank, topics is a nonempty array whose clauses are arrays and whose combined clause set is nonempty with unique nonblank ids. Use semanticIdOf("metroplat.artifact") from the exact original Data supplier, as the incumbent EAVT identity facade does, to obtain the original Requirement address. The supplier interface is CURRIED: semanticIdOf(objectKind) returns a function, then that function takes the complete parsed Requirement JSON object and returns its canonical ni URI string. The import subpath is @red-cup-engineering/computer-data-engineering-services-section/semiotic-expression-notation/semantic-content-identity. Never substitute raw JSON SHA hashing or reimplement its SEN envelope codec. These extra complete Requirement/address fixtures were obtained by executing that exact installed original supplier, so independent finite assay cases can construct changed-current source cuts without repository access or hash computation: [{"kind":"unhyphenated","requirement":{"id":"fixture","kind":"requirement","met-when":[{"cell":"fixture:retain","target":"held","says":"Retain the value."}]},"address":"ni:///sha-256;sYm8anaGyMI9pbVbKoJmI7tFP-vzSWIuJy6yzjBGcG4"},{"kind":"unicode-separators","requirement":{"id":"fixture-requirement","kind":"requirement","met-when":[{"cell":"fixture-requirement:retain","target":"held","says":"Retain\u0085the\u2028value\u2029."}]},"address":"ni:///sha-256;q_KZEY192wMV7O3tK8Zm9SvWb_0M91NFPxhUxHVphDU"}]. The displayed extra fixtures are standard JSON: parse their JSON escapes normally. The unicode-separators fixture Requirement.says contains actual Unicode NEXT LINE, LINE SEPARATOR and PARAGRAPH SEPARATOR characters, not literal backslash-u sequences. Its supplied address was computed from that actual-character Requirement object. The outer fixture kind/address wrapper is not part of the Requirement being identified. Source texts are then ordinary JSON.stringify serializations of their selected parsed record; plan byte hashes always bind the exact text actually supplied, including its chosen JSON escape spelling. The additional two-cell public source-cut examples carry complete native Requirement/Specification records, exact current texts/hashes and source-bound HLD/LLD plan/candidate fixtures. They are declarative synthetic examples, never generated production designs, tests or adoption. Their complete Requirement address, computed by the original supplier, is ni:///sha-256;OEj5bHpv9i9Jgius2JNA_LKW0fPwUOTQ15pt_GBqSuY. Their paired native Specification address is ni:///sha-256;apjra6hyFRIOI13O8QSRiCbdxX-rVfiMKPFzYaDZbgo. Cross-row ID reuse is valid in those fixtures; duplicate IDs within any one trace list remain invalid. Only exact byte hashes for plan sources use node:crypto. Specification.identity and Specification.provenance are nonnull nonarray JSON objects; every topics entry and every combined clauses entry is also a nonnull nonarray JSON object, with their stated array/string fields checked before membership access. provenance.derivedFrom is an array of strings and has an element exactly equal to requirement.id plus @ plus that address; a scalar string, substring or coercible value is not the array carrier and yields TypeError. Other derivedFrom string entries may be blank or repeated in this structural projection; the original full upstream validator remains separately required. Architecture.identity and every models entry are likewise nonnull nonarray JSON objects with the stated exact nonblank string fields. Missing, null, wrong-type or malformed nested carrier fields in this projection yield TypeError, never a default or coercion. Each clause backedBy is a nonempty unique array of the exact current incumbent references: Original Requirement <requirement.id> (<address>), cell <cell>. Every cell has at least one backing clause and no foreign/stale backing reference is admitted by this projection. Architecture.type is ArchitectureDescriptionCandidate, identity.id and identity.revision are nonblank, and models is a nonempty array with unique nonblank ids and nonblank content. Unlisted fields are permitted, retained in exact source text and otherwise ignored by this projection. This is not full upstream schema/standard/adoption validation: the original caller must separately run the incumbent full source-cut validator. [claims: bootstrap-scope, declared-interface, data-supplier]
- `source-binding`: Sort sources in requirement,specification,architecture,high-level order, preserving each exact text. Construct every resulting source object anew with own keys inserted exactly kind, then text, then digest, regardless of the original input object key order. These canonical-key source objects are used both for plan.sources and for the exact JSON.stringify of the user message payload; the parent payload inserts stage then sources. No input object insertion order leaks into that generated message string. This canonicalizes the new envelope only; never reserialize or normalize the retained source text strings themselves. Bind each text with ordinary sha256: plus lowercase SHA-256 UTF8 hex using node:crypto. These are exact-byte hashes, not a new Semiotic Content Address codec. Planning and receiving produce the same declared JSON data for the same input; source-level absence of clock and host effects has separate mandatory review. [claims: bootstrap-scope, declared-interface]
- `native-request`: For each valid plan, return type EngineeringDesignDerivationPlan, stage, sources with their exact text/digest, and request. request.taskClass is code-fabrication, objective is Derive high-level design or Derive low-level design, requiredCapabilities is [text-output], schema is the declared corresponding HLD or LLD schema. messages is exactly system then user: system content is "Derive only the requested engineering design candidate from the complete original source cut. Retain every original requirement, explicit uncertainty and source identity. Follow the supplied response schema. A candidate is not adoption, proof, fabrication, installation or operation."; user content is JSON.stringify of the object with fields stage then sources, using the sorted digest-bearing sources. These plans are consumed by the original infer carrier; the module never calls a model, signs, purchases or executes. [claims: bootstrap-scope, declared-interface]
- `hld-profile`: The HLD design schema is exactly {"type":"object","additionalProperties":false,"required":["components","connections","traceability","openQuestions"],"properties":{"components":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"object","additionalProperties":false,"required":["id","owner","layer","responsibility","dependencyIds","providedPorts","requiredPorts"],"properties":{"id":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"owner":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"layer":{"enum":["data","code","networking","systems"]},"responsibility":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"dependencyIds":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1}},"providedPorts":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1}},"requiredPorts":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1}}}},"minItems":1},"connections":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"object","additionalProperties":false,"required":["fromComponentId","toComponentId","port"],"properties":{"fromComponentId":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"toComponentId":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"port":{"type":"string","minLength":1}}}},"traceability":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"object","additionalProperties":false,"required":["requirementCellId","specificationClauseIds","architectureModelIds","designElementIds"],"properties":{"requirementCellId":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"specificationClauseIds":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"minItems":1},"architectureModelIds":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"minItems":1},"designElementIds":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"minItems":1}}},"minItems":1},"openQuestions":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1}}}}. Component ids are globally unique within the design. Each individual dependencyIds, providedPorts and requiredPorts array contains unique nonblank strings. A port spelling may repeat across components or across the provided/required arrays; its actual provider and consumer are identified by the connection endpoints. Each dependency and connection endpoint must name an existing distinct component. A dependency may exist without a port connection; every connection must satisfy its dependency/port rules. The exact layers from lower to higher are data, code, networking, systems. Dependencies are acyclic and follow Data -> Code -> Networking -> Systems: a component may use the same or a lower layer, never a higher layer. Each connection is directed fromComponentId=provider and toComponentId=consumer. The consumer dependencyIds must include that provider id, the provider providedPorts must contain the connection port and the consumer requiredPorts must contain it. Each required port has exactly one such provider connection. A providedPorts entry may be unused with no consumer. Components may have no ports, disconnected dependency subgraphs are allowed, and every component is still covered by complete-trace. Reject duplicate connection triples, multiple connections for the same consumer required port, missing providers for a required port, or any connection violating the stated endpoint/dependency/provided/required rules. These are the exhaustive connection rules; no whole-graph connectivity or every-provided-port-use rule is imposed. [claims: bootstrap-scope, declared-interface]
- `lld-profile`: The LLD design schema is exactly {"type":"object","additionalProperties":false,"required":["operations","traceability","openQuestions"],"properties":{"operations":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"object","additionalProperties":false,"required":["id","componentId","module","exportName","inputSchemaJson","outputSchemaJson","behavior","preconditions","postconditions","failureCases","resourceBounds","effectBoundaries","acceptanceObligations"],"properties":{"id":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"componentId":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"module":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"exportName":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"inputSchemaJson":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"outputSchemaJson":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"behavior":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"preconditions":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1}},"postconditions":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"minItems":1},"failureCases":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"minItems":1},"resourceBounds":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"minItems":1},"effectBoundaries":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"minItems":1},"acceptanceObligations":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"minItems":1}}},"minItems":1},"traceability":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"object","additionalProperties":false,"required":["requirementCellId","specificationClauseIds","architectureModelIds","designElementIds"],"properties":{"requirementCellId":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"specificationClauseIds":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"minItems":1},"architectureModelIds":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"minItems":1},"designElementIds":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1},"minItems":1}}},"minItems":1},"openQuestions":{"type":"array","items":{"type":"string","minLength":1}}}}. Each operation id and module/exportName pair is unique. Each operation.componentId names a component in the supplied HLD. Modules stay under src/, end in .mjs and contain no absolute/empty/dot/dotdot/backslash segments. exportName is a public module export name restricted to the exact ASCII spelling pattern ^[A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$]*$, with no escapes or additional keyword exclusion. This is whole-string ASCII membership: the first character is an ASCII letter, underscore or dollar, and every later character is an ASCII letter, digit, underscore or dollar. Do not trim the exportName, enable multiline mode or allow any trailing line terminator/whitespace. An exportName with final LF, CR, CRLF, Unicode LINE SEPARATOR or PARAGRAPH SEPARATOR returns invalid-design on LLD delivery. The actual installed Node runtime observations for the non-multiline literal regex are [{"spelling":"retain","admitted":true},{"spelling":"retain\n","admitted":false},{"spelling":"retain\r","admitted":false},{"spelling":"retain\r\n","admitted":false},{"spelling":"retain\u2028","admitted":false},{"spelling":"retain\u2029","admitted":false}]. It is not a local BindingIdentifier: class, default, await, eval and arguments are admitted export-name spellings at this candidate boundary when every other LLD condition holds. The original Code maker owns a valid local binding/export alias and actual module syntax qualification; this design receiver neither generates that code nor certifies syntax. inputSchemaJson and outputSchemaJson parse as nonnull nonarray JSON objects or booleans, with exact original spelling retained; schema semantic qualification remains with the incumbent Code/JSON Schema maker. Every HLD component is realized by at least one operation. Behavioral postconditions, failures, proposed finite resource bounds, effect boundaries and independent acceptance obligations are explicit design claims. Their machine-checked source correspondence is only the complete-trace IDs; this module does not establish the claims are true, supported by measurements or semantically refined. Structurally valid but semantically unsupported statements therefore remain readable unadmitted candidates with proof unreceived, not invalid-design for an unspecified semantic criterion. Actual bound qualification and semantic review are separate downstream obligations, never inferred from nonblank strings. [claims: bootstrap-scope, declared-interface]
- `complete-trace`: Design traceability carries exactly one row for every original Requirement met-when cell. Each row specificationClauseIds is exactly the set of all original Specification topic-clause ids whose exact incumbent backedBy reference names that cell, in any order. Each row architectureModelIds selects a nonempty set of existing Architecture models; designElementIds must name actual components for HLD or actual operations for LLD. Every design element occurs in at least one row. Each row specificationClauseIds, architectureModelIds and designElementIds is nonempty and internally unique; a repeated id in one of these arrays returns invalid-design even though the JSON schema alone permits it. IDs may reappear across different rows. Duplicate rows mean repeated requirementCellId and are invalid. No foreign, missing or empty trace is accepted. For LLD, the componentIds of each row selected operations must equal the original HLD row designElementIds as a set, so every HLD allocation for that same cell is refined. This verifies correspondence, not semantic refinement or institutional adoption. [claims: bootstrap-scope, declared-interface]
- `lld-upstream`: The supplied high-level source for LLD is an EngineeringDesignCandidate with stage high-level, admitted false, proof unreceived, a valid HLD profile and exact matching requirement/specification/architecture texts/digests. Its parsed envelope has exactly type, stage, sources, design, admitted and proof, with no extra fields. Its sources entries each have exactly kind, text and digest, and its sources array must equal the reconstructed current high-level plan sources in canonical requirement,specification,architecture order; object key order is ignored, array order and all strings are exact. A nested HLD sources permutation, duplicate, missing/extra source, changed digest, changed original text, wrong envelope or invalid HLD/trace causes TypeError as an invalid upstream source. The outer operation input sources are separately sorted by kind, so permuting only those outer entries is accepted and produces the same canonical plan. A candidate with changed original source must not enter LLD as if current. The candidate remains conditional; separately qualified Proof and adoption are owed outside this module. [claims: bootstrap-scope, declared-interface]
- `receive-original`: receiveEngineeringDesign accepts fields plan, stage, sources, deliveryText. The receive envelope must have exactly plan, stage, sources and deliveryText with their declared types; plan must be a nonnull nonarray JSON object. Any such plan object, including {} or an object missing request, is admitted at the envelope boundary and compared to the reconstructed complete current plan; it is not TypeError merely because plan fields are absent or changed. First reconstruct the plan from current stage/sources and compare its complete JSON data semantically to the supplied plan, including exact source texts, hashes and native inference request. A changed or forged plan returns exactly {type:EngineeringDesignDerivationRefusal,reason:source-cut-mismatch}. Otherwise parse deliveryText as one JSON value and receive only the selected HLD or LLD profile with all reference/layer/trace checks. Invalid/malformed/incomplete design returns exactly {type:EngineeringDesignDerivationRefusal,reason:invalid-design}. Return a valid candidate with exactly type EngineeringDesignCandidate, stage, the plan sources, unchanged parsed design, admitted false, proof unreceived. No repair/defaulting of a delivery is allowed. Plan comparison ignores object key order but preserves array order and all string spelling. Compare JSON primitive types without coercion; numbers use ordinary numeric equality (JavaScript ===), so numerically equal numbers compare equal and zero equals negative zero at this plan comparison boundary. This does not change the separate native Data supplier refusal for negative zero inside an original Requirement. A schema minimum changed from one to zero or negative zero is unequal and returns source-cut-mismatch. Objects require exactly the same own key set and recursively equal corresponding values; arrays require equal length and recursively equal same-index values, distinct from objects; null equals only null. There is no numeric-string coercion, Unicode normalization or semantic interpretation of strings. All design strings constrained by minLength1 must also be nonblank; ids/references are compared by exact spelling without normalization. Duplicate plain-text lists are allowed unless an explicit id/port/trace rule forbids them. The listed schemas and explicit rules are exhaustive for structural admission: do not invent extra semantic refusal conditions. [claims: bootstrap-scope, declared-interface]
- `unknowns-and-independence`: Received design.openQuestions is retained verbatim, including nonempty unknowns and duplicate questions; no natural-language mapping from upstream question objects is imposed here, and all original questions remain in the exact retained source texts. A structurally complete candidate with questions remains readable but unadmitted; downstream effectful fabrication must separately refuse blocking unknowns and require source/design-pinned Proof. Semantic refinement, truth of design/bound claims, full upstream standard conformance and competent source adoption are all unassessed by these operations, even when IDs and all structural conditions match. The independent-channel and source/artifact obligations have separate mandatory native review; no functional case is asserted to verify them. [claims: bootstrap-scope, declared-interface]
## Prohibitions
- `no-false-closure`: Never emit admitted true, accepted/qualified proof, a self-acceptance, a source-adoption receipt, generated application code or any installation/earning claim. This prohibition governs assertions and effects owned by the generator, not the natural-language testimony preserved inside design or original sources. A structurally valid LLD behavior, postcondition, bound, effect statement or question containing an unsupported installation, proof or earning claim remains unchanged inside an unadmitted candidate with proof unreceived. No keyword or unspecified semantic-content filter returns invalid-design for such text; it is retained testimony, never endorsed as fact or executable normative application code. The same distinction covers quoted code/prospective effect descriptions in those strings. Their truth, semantic refinement, competent adoption and effect authority remain separate mandatory downstream gates. Complete source/design retention here means unchanged current sources and the full parsed design under the stated structural/reference checks, not a new natural-language completeness test. Do not silently omit requirements, sources, questions, operations or effects from the receiving result. [claims: bootstrap-scope, declared-interface]
- `bounded-domain`: Use only the declared finite JSON inputs. Outer input-envelope validation remains mandatory but its receiving is the separate outer-envelope-receiving review; the native case carrier admits only inputSchema-valid case values and must not be widened or fed outside-schema envelopes. In-domain source-text decoding and plan/design refusals remain native functional assay obligations. First validate the complete operation envelope against its declared inputSchema, including additionalProperties, required fields, enum, array/string minima and nested source fields; any violation is TypeError. deliveryText is declared only as a string, with no minLength. A non-string deliveryText is TypeError; any string, including the empty string or whitespace, passes the envelope type check. After successful current plan comparison, failure to parse that string as valid selected candidate JSON returns invalid-design. A mismatching plan is refused as source-cut-mismatch before inspecting delivery content. Any JSON object as plan is allowed by that input schema; its missing or changed inner fields are handled by complete plan comparison, never TypeError alone. Refuse malformed source records, invalid stage/sources/deliveryText and a nonobject/null/array plan with TypeError. A well-typed receive envelope carrying any JSON object as plan proceeds to complete plan comparison and a source-cut-mismatch refusal when unequal; candidate content refusals use the two explicit reason values. The host/typed request owner retains byte, time and resource limits; do not invent successful bounds or default missing design choices. [claims: bootstrap-scope, declared-interface]

## Standards
- `native-line` | Red Cup Engineering | installed Fabrication0.4.15-dev.1 and JSNodePackage0.4.0 | /gnu/store/sq5kwc4b6y2k7h7izqi6rjbadjvvagwy-bare-cedar-fog-deployment-1/node_modules/@red-cup-engineering/computer-systems-fabrication-engineering-services-section/scripts/fabricate-jsnode-closure.mjs | Applies to the original source-free module meaning closure and isolated native receiver. | The Code maker derives source and independent clause assays, projects native ESM exports, runs the complete isolated module set and preserves its actual lot outcome before a proposal is received. Supplier delivery guidance for this order: if refusing derivation, enumerate all blocking specification questions identified in this cut together in the one question string, instead of one known question per invocation. - `export-name-grammar` | Ecma TC39 | living draft observed2026-10-27, ModuleExportName and IdentifierName productions only | https://tc39.es/ecma262/multipage/ecmascript-language-scripts-and-modules.html#prod-ModuleExportName | Applies only to distinguishing a public export name from a local binding; this contract further restricts the spelling to ASCII. | ModuleExportName admits IdentifierName; this does not make a reserved word a valid local binding or claim whole-edition conformance.

## Modules

## Acceptance obligations
```json
[{"id":"stage-contract-receiving","kind":"assay","statement":"A distinct pre-source native assay derivation covers both stages, source/order mutation represented by changed input values, invalid JSON/type/duplicates, forged plans, complete positive HLD/LLD, source drift, layer violation/cycle/ports, trace omission/foreign IDs, LLD component coverage/module/schema parsing, nonempty unknown retention and candidate claim limits. Cover every in-domain behavioral requirement/prohibition through the unchanged operation/input/output/throws carrier. Case input must satisfy the declared operation input schema. Source text can still carry malformed decoded records for TypeError cases. Invalid outer operation envelopes are outside this carrier and must not be encoded as if admitted cases; their mandatory TypeError receiving is separately owned by outer-envelope-receiving. Do not attach unsupported observation fields or pretend those cases verify source placement, host-effect absence, original export preservation, channel independence or post-call unchanged nested inputs; those remain mandatory separate review obligations."},{"id":"original-consumer-receiving","kind":"review","statement":"The original Engineering caller consumes the produced request through its actual existing infer carrier and passes that original delivery to receiving, producing real HLD then LLD candidates for the Moneyday generation/hygiene requirement. Publish and install in the existing Specification owner only after native original-producer tests and independent installed consumer receiving; this work order itself grants no publication, proof or operational acceptance."},{"id":"source-artifact-and-channel-receiving","kind":"review","statement":"- `existing-owner-placement`: Produce only src/engineering-design.mjs with the two named exports planEngineeringDesign and receiveEngineeringDesign for the original red-cup-engineering/specification-engineering-services-section. Preserve all incumbent Specification/Architecture operations. This bootstrap does not add a package, direct runtime service, alternate enterprise board, clock, signer or earning controller. [claims: bootstrap-scope, declared-interface] Preserve all incumbent source/export bytes and consumers through original-owner integration. Independently inspect the actual generated artifact with the original Code parser/module/closure readers and read the native fabrication lots/proofing: writer receives only original work order/examples, never derived hidden assays. Functional clause tags do not establish this review. No artifact is qualified for consumer use until this review is received."},{"id":"host-effects-and-input-custody-receiving","kind":"review","statement":"- `no-effects`: No network, environment, filesystem reads/writes, credentials, subprocess, clock, random state, package installation/publication or execution in either operation. node:crypto hashing and native JSON parsing are the only non-domain primitives needed. Do not mutate any input, nested value or declaration. [claims: bootstrap-scope, declared-interface] Independently inspect actual source/call effects and argument ownership, alongside real native isolation and consumer execution. Do not infer no mutation or no host effect from an output-only case or report an unreceived review as passed. Ordinary downstream pre-fabrication Proof/adoption remain separate."},{"id":"outer-envelope-receiving","kind":"review","statement":"The bounded-domain outer operation input-schema validation and required TypeError behavior are mandatory. The unchanged functional assay carrier itself excludes missing/extra envelope fields, invalid stage, non-string deliveryText and nonobject plan, so its cases cannot establish this obligation. Independently inspect the actual generated exports against every declared outer input field/schema constraint and receive the actual original caller through its existing native typed schema admission. Preserve all required refusals; never treat this review as passed from valid-input clause tags, schema declaration alone or unreceived author testimony. No bootstrap producer is qualified for original consumer use until this boundary receiving is received."}]
```
